To study these challenges, we employ three kinds of prompts namely, (i) Direct Query Prompt (ii) Context-Guided Prompt, and (iii) Exemplary Context Prompt that incorporate different levels of diabetes dietary guidelines from medical sources. We introduce a benchmark dataset curated for this investigation consisting of 7607 recipes that include 3807 recipes suitable for diabetes and 3800 recipes not suitable for diabetes. Our results demonstrate that most LLMs are cautious in predicting recipes as suitable to prevent detrimental outcomes. Further, the models that can reason using the dietary guidelines performed better in predicting the suitability of recipes for diabetes. Overall, Mistral-7B and Llama 70B showed superior performance to their counterparts.

</details>


### [169] [Alignment-Free Text-Audiobox for Voice Dubbing and Full-Duplex Dialogue Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03992)
*Sanyuan Chen,Min-Jae Hwang,Sho Inoue,Anna Sun,Bokai Yu,David Kant,Dongmin Hyun,Dorian Desblancs,Gregory Antonovsky,Oleg Repin,Peng-Jen Chen,Xutai Ma,Zehai Tu,Juan Pino,Wei-Ning Hsu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Alignment-Free Text-Audiobox (Text-AB)，一个统一的语音配音与全双工对话合成框架。它基于扩散Transformer和流匹配目标训练，在潜在扩散框架中使用DAC-VAE特征（将48kHz波形压缩为25Hz潜在序列，压缩比超10倍），无需强制对齐和显式时长预测，通过跨注意力学习文本-语音对齐。3B参数模型在48万小时单语语音上预训练，随后针对跨语言配音、全双工对话合成及情感对话合成三个下游任务进行监督微调。推理时支持约1分钟一次性生成和长文生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统配音和对话合成系统依赖强制对齐和显式时长预测，流程复杂且限制扩展性；同时语音表征压缩效率与重建质量之间存在权衡。作者旨在构建一个无需对齐标注、具备高压缩比且可扩展的统一框架，覆盖配音、对话及情感表达等多项任务。

Method: Text-AB基于流匹配目标训练的扩散Transformer。区别于Audiobox的三点：(1) 采用DAC-VAE特征，将48kHz波形编码为25Hz潜在序列，压缩率比EnCodec高10倍以上且重建质量更优；(2) 无对齐设计：使用现成文本编码器处理原始文本，通过跨注意力机制学习文本-语音对齐，免去强制对齐和显式时长预测；(3) 规模扩展：3B参数模型预训练于48万小时单语语音，再在三个下游任务上进行监督微调。推理时支持单次生成约1分钟语音，并通过多扩散方案支持任意长度生成，配合多阶段重排序策略提升质量。

Result: 在真实世界配音基准上，Text-AB相比最新内部配音系统在韵律相似性、声音相似性、自然度和可分享性上均有大幅提升。在全双工对话合成方面，短对话接近人类录音水平，长对话在类人性和表现力上显著优于最新内部模型，并原生建模了话轮转换、反馈语和情感动态。情感对话合成中，情感条件化显著提高了情感对齐和情感交互质量。

Conclusion: Text-AB验证了去除强制对齐和显式时长预测的无对齐范式在高压缩率语音表示下可达到领先性能，统一框架能够同时支持配音、对话和情感对话等多任务，且在规模化预训练基础上通过任务微调实现各场景的显著质量提升。

Abstract: We present Alignment-Free Text-Audiobox (Text-AB), a unified framework for high-quality voice dubbing and full-duplex dialogue synthesis. Building on a Diffusion Transformer trained with a flow-matching objective, Text-AB departs from the Audiobox system along three dimensions. First, it operates in a latent diffusion framework using DAC-VAE features that encode 48 kHz waveforms into a 25 Hz latent sequence, giving over 10x higher compression than previous EnCodec representations while improving resynthesis quality. Second, Text-AB is alignment-free: it consumes raw text via an off-the-shelf text encoder and learns text-speech alignment through cross-attention, removing the need for forced alignment and explicit duration prediction. Third, we scale model and data substantially, pretraining a 3B-parameter model on 480k hours of monolingual speech, followed by supervised fine-tuning on three downstream tasks: cross-lingual voice dubbing, full-duplex dialogue synthesis, and emotional full-duplex dialogue synthesis. At inference, Text-AB supports one-shot generation for up to ~1 min of speech and arbitrarily long-form generation via a multi-diffusion scheme, plus a multi-stage reranking strategy that enhances quality based on automated metrics. On a real-world dubbing benchmark, Text-AB delivers a step-change improvement over the latest internal dubbing system, with large gains in prosody similarity, voice similarity, naturalness, and shareability. For full-duplex dialogue synthesis, it approaches human recordings on short-form conversations and substantially outperforms the latest internal model on long-form human-likeness and expressivity, while natively modeling turn-taking, back-channeling, and emotional dynamics. For emotional dialogue synthesis, emotion conditioning significantly improves emotion alignment and emotional interaction quality over the unconditioned baseline.

</details>


### [170] [Representational alignment yields generalizable safety in language models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04022)
*Lingyu Li,Yan Teng,Yingchun Wang,Xia Hu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现当前LLM对道德概念的原型分类能力较弱，并提出"表征相似性优化"方法，直接对齐模型潜在表征与人类道德判断中的分类结构，而非监督生成响应。该方法在显式道德判断上提升有限，但在多种对抗基准和攻击策略下一致提升了模型的鲁棒性，为原型分类促进行为适应性提供了功能支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM对齐方法主要优化可观察的响应，但模型在面对以不熟悉或对抗形式重述的相同有害意图时仍易受攻击。原型理论认为人类概念以中心案例为代表、按典型性分级分类，这种能力可能是人类道德判断适应性的来源，而当前LLM是否保留了这种分类结构尚不清楚。

Method: 研究先对23个LLM进行道德概念原型分类能力的系统性评估，发现模型常无法区分对立道德类别或保留类别内部的细粒度典型性排序。随后提出表征相似性优化（representational similarity optimization），直接对齐LLM潜在表征与人类道德判断所表达的分类结构，而非在响应层面进行监督。在匹配实验中，与使用相同的251,334条道德标注的标准行为对齐进行比较。

Result: 标准行为对齐能在响应层面学习到预期的道德判断，但分类结构基本未变，且增加了多种对抗评估下的脆弱性；而表征相似性优化虽然在显式判断提升上较温和，但在不同模型规模、多样基准和攻击策略下一致改善了对抗鲁棒性。缺陷在参数规模和对齐阶段中持续存在。

Conclusion: 原型式分类对行为适应性有功能贡献，将该表征原理迁移到LLM可在对抗条件下获得可泛化的安全性。这为超越响应级对齐、面向表征结构对齐的模型安全方法提供了理论与实证支持。

Abstract: Aligning large language models (LLMs) is essential for their safe deployment. Current alignment methods mainly optimize observable responses, yet models remain vulnerable when the same harmful intent is recast in unfamiliar or adversarial forms that humans can easily recognize. Prototype theory offers an account of this adaptability. Human concepts are represented around central cases, and new instances are categorized according to their graded typicality relative to these prototypes. Here we show that such categorization of moral concepts is weakly preserved in current LLMs. Across 23 LLMs, models often failed to distinguish opposed moral categories or preserve fine-grained typicality within each category. These deficits persist across parameter sizes and alignment stages. We developed representational similarity optimization, which directly aligns the latent representations in LLMs with the categorization expressed in human moral judgements, without supervising generated responses. In matched experiments using the same 251,334 moral annotations, standard behavioral alignment learned the intended moral judgements at the response level while leaving the categorization structure largely unchanged and increasing vulnerability across adversarial evaluations. Reorganizing moral categorization produced more modest gains in explicit judgements but consistently improved adversarial robustness across model scales on diverse benchmarks and attack strategies. Our findings provide functional support for the view that prototype-based categorization contributes to behavioral adaptability. They also show that transferring this representational principle to LLMs yields generalizable safety under adversarial conditions.

</details>


### [171] [Translation as a Decision Space: A Multi-Agent Perspective on Low-Resource Dialect Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04048)
*Hasan Alkhder,Mohammad Abboush,Igor Tchappi,Ahmet Zengin,Amro Najjar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将神经机器翻译重新定义为由自主翻译智能体探索的结构化决策空间，并通过土耳其语到叙利亚阿拉伯语的低资源方言翻译实验，用三种智能体（零样本直译、方言稳定化微调、英语枢轴翻译）来量化翻译分歧作为可解释的行为信号，而非将其视为错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NMT系统通常为每个输入只生成单一输出，掩盖了多语言解码中潜在的可选决策路径。这种不透明性在低资源方言场景中尤为突出，因为多种语言学上有效的实现方式可能在词汇真实性、语域和结构稳定性上存在差异。作者认为智能体间的分歧不应被视为错误，而应作为可解释的行为信号来揭示多语言模型内部的潜在决策灵活性。

Method: 将翻译重构为多智能体系统，在一个共享的多语言骨干上运行三个不同路径的翻译智能体：(1)零样本直译、(2)通过轻量级微调实现方言稳定的翻译、(3)通过英语的枢轴翻译。在5,000个电视对话及MADAR-Turk资源的土耳其语-叙利亚阿拉伯语句对上训练稳定化，并在5,000句对话上评测。采用方言标记频率、与标准阿拉伯语的词汇接近度和结构方差来量化结构化行为位移，而非传统性能指标。

Result: 轻量级稳定化使方言标记使用率从0.2266翻倍至0.4988，同时显著降低结构不稳定性。枢轴翻译引入规范化压力和可测的压缩效应，而零样本直译表现出最高的决策方差。

Conclusion: 翻译智能体之间的分歧揭示了多语言模型中潜在的决策灵活性，作者为低资源方言生成提供了一个原则性的可解释性框架，将分歧转化为可量化的行为信号。

Abstract: Neural machine translation (NMT) systems typically produce a single output per input, obscuring the alternative decision trajectories implicitly available within multilingual decoding. This opacity becomes particularly problematic in low-resource dialect settings, where multiple linguistically valid realizations may differ in lexical authenticity, register, and structural stability. We propose reframing translation as a structured decision space explored by autonomous translation agents. Instead of analyzing a single output, we model distinct translation pathways as agents operating over a shared multilingual backbone. Inter-agent divergence is treated not as error but as an interpretable behavioral signal. We conduct an empirical study on Turkish--Syrian Arabic translation using three agents: (1) zero-shot direct translation, (2) dialect-stabilized translation via lightweight fine-tuning, and (3) pivot translation through English. Evaluation is performed on 5,000 dialogue sentences, while stabilization is trained on 5,000 additional Turkish--Syrian sentence pairs drawn from television dialogue and MADAR-Turk resources. Rather than optimizing for conventional performance metrics, we quantify structured behavioral displacement using dialect marker frequency, lexical proximity to standardized Arabic, and structural variance. Lightweight stabilization nearly doubles dialect marker usage, increasing it from 0.2266 to 0.4988, while significantly reducing structural instability. Pivot mediation introduces normalization pressure and measurable compression effects, whereas zero-shot translation exhibits the highest decision variance. We argue that translation divergence across agents reveals latent decision flexibility within multilingual models and we provide a principled interpretability framework for low-resource dialect generation.

</details>


### [172] [Sequential Beats Joint: On the Interplay between On-Policy Distillation and RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04108)
*Boyan Li,Bingsen Chen,Chenghao Yang,Ping Nie,Chen Zhao,Xi Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个简单的两阶段方案——OPD-then-RL，即在逻辑与数学推理基准上先进行on-policy蒸馏（OPD）再使用带可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR），该方案稳定地优于纯OPD、纯RLVR以及所有将两者单步融合的联合基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中已有工作尝试将OPD的稠密token级监督与RL的稀疏奖励在单步内融合（加权相加或教师调制缩放RL优势），但作者认为这种纠缠式融合存在信号干扰问题，需要用更清晰的分阶段方式来结合两种方法并给出系统性理解。

Method: 提出OPD-then-RL两阶段训练方案：第一阶段进行on-policy distillation扩展学生对教师支持解的覆盖范围，第二阶段切换到带可验证奖励的强化学习在支持集内锐化策略。通过pass@k行为、学习动力学与参数更新三个角度系统分析各方法的差异。

Result: 两阶段方案在逻辑和数学推理基准上一致超越纯OPD、纯RLVR及所有联合融合基线；实验发现OPD验证分数是切换至RL的关键信号，且OPD作为RL的冷启动优于SFT。

Conclusion: OPD-then-RL将两个相互干扰的纠缠信号转化为互补的阶段：OPD决定"覆盖哪些解"（breadth），RL决定"在支持内选哪个解"（sharpness），构建了一种简单而强大的后训练推理模型组合范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) and on-policy distillation (OPD) have emerged as two dominant methods for post-training reasoning LLMs. Prior work uses OPD's dense token-level supervision to complement the sparse RL reward, fusing the two signals within a single step: either as a \emph{weighted-additive combination} or a \emph{teacher-modulated rescaling} of the RL advantage. In this paper, we show that a simple two-stage scheme, OPD-then-RL, consistently outperforms pure OPD, pure RLVR, and all such joint baselines across logic and math reasoning benchmarks. Beyond the empirical results, we further provide a systematic understanding of this through pass@$k$ behavior, learning dynamics, and parameter updates, yielding a consistent explanation: OPD expands the student's coverage of teacher-supported solutions and RL sharpens within that support, while jointly optimizing the two signals causes them to interfere.To provide a practical recipe, we find that the OPD validation score is the key signal for when to switch to RL, and that OPD is a better cold start for RL than SFT. Together, our results establish OPD-then-RL as a simple yet strong way to combine the two methods, turning two entangled signals into complementary stages.

</details>


### [173] [Last Translation Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04173)
*Vilém Zouhar,Niyati Bafna,Mukund Choudhary,Maike Züfle,Sara Rajaee,Pinzhen Chen,Jannis Vamvas,Sara Papi,Ona de Gibert,Bhavitvya Malik,Eliya Habba,Orfeas Menis Mastromichalakis,Patrícia Schmidtová,Michelle Wastl,Sheriff Issaka,Leshem Choshen,Stella Biderman,Antonis Anastasopoulos,Jan Niehues,Rico Sennrich,Mrinmaya Sachan,Ondřej Bojar,Kenton Murray,Jörg Tiedemann,Alham Fikri Aji,Philipp Koehn,Christof Monz,Alexandra Birch,Sowmya Vajjala,Chalamalasetti Kranti,Cristina España-Bonet,Nobin Sarwar,David Kaczér,Shunta Asano,Malik Marmonier,Daban Q. Jaff,Vaisakhi Mishra,Hend Al- Khalifa,Gabriele Sarti,Sourajit Saha,Nils Rehlinger,Juan Daniel Cuervo Villa,Jonathan Tonglet,Saugata Purkayastha,Dominik Macháček,Jagannathan Ramanujam,Heejin Do,Zuzana Nadova,Fred Philippy,Fabian Retkowski,Maria Lymperaiou,Silvia Casola,Hanna Yukhymenko,Shubhashis Roy Dipta,Sangwon Ryu,Andrés Jerez,Ron Keinan,Shuaib Shuaib Yusuf,Avantica Vempati,Maria Carmen Staiano,Sukannya Purkayastha,Adrian Cosma,Vitalii Babenko,Erivan Inan,Aviral Nigam,Wafa Aissa,Fatima Haouari,Venkata Prasanth Kumar Gummadi,Mehdi Jafarzadeh,Valentin Scourneau,Lukas Edman,Kaiser Sun,Shaomu Tan,Mohammad Sadegh Gholizadeh,Johannes-Rudolf David,Dipankar Srirag,Javier García Gilabert,Ruta Binkyte,Manar Ali,Ana-Maria Bucur,Sabry E. Farrag,Youssef Saber,Yihong Liu,Jean Maillard,Cojocaru Nicoleta,Xiaochuang Yuan,Sina Ahmadi,Philipp Mondorf,Kaustubh Dhole,Roman Wixinger,Shenbin Qian,Manuel Tuor,Sergey Troshin,Jonathan Yahav,Fida Mohammad Thoker,Amir Arsalan Rezapour,Lance Calvin Lim Gamboa,Manon Reusens,Kätriin Kukk,Koel Dutta Chowdhury,Giuseppe Gallipoli,Christian Hoang,Shaswati Saha,Seth Aycock,Jan Kocoń,Bo Chen,Linh Vu,Vatsal Venkatkrishna,Arafat Ahsan,Luan Thanh Nguyen,Hassan Soliman,Daryna Dementieva,Theresia Veronika Rampisela,Ngoc Quynh Tram Do,Marius Huber,Kazuki Egashira,Azmine Toushik Wasi,Vladislav Poritski,Mike Zhang,Deep Shah,Paul Gavrikov,Luis Frentzen Salim,David Africa,R. Damanhuri,Bello Umar Bello,Anumit Garg,Gengyu Rao,Pawan Sasanka Ammanamanchi,Kamile Dementaviciute,Andrianos Michail,L D M S Sai Teja,Dawei Zhu,Yi Fan,Wei Liu,Farhan Farsi,Elias Herranen,Sankalan Pal Chowdhury,Karen Sanchez,Farzad Shami,Ashok Urlana,Zimu Wang,Tomasz Limisiewicz,Priyaranjan Pattnayak,Marii Ojastu,Hongbin Na,Emilian Radoi,Chenyi Zhao,Carlos Hinojosa,Andrea Gregor de Varda,Zaid Alyafeai,Reem Alzahrani,Nehal Kathrotia,Alex Flückiger,Ulysses Sekai Tully Carr,Jimson Paulo Layacan,Guy Kaplan,Ritwik Tiwari,Rishit Dagli,Oksana Volchek,Isaac R Caswell,Bowen Yi,Blanka Kövér,Amir Hossein Yari,Aicha Chorana,Zhengxiang Wang,Selja Keränen,Samuel Simko,Joy Olusanya,Jenny Chim,Enzo Doyen,Vivek Harsha Lakkamaneni,Sophia Conrad,Pouya Sadeghi,Panayiotis Panayiotou,Luis Lara,Jannatul Nayem,Eran Yahav,Debanshu Das,Antonia Karamolegkou,Anmol Goel,Aishik Mandal,Tommaso Cerruti,Raoyuan Zhao,Mykola Haltiuk,Thura Aung,Naser Almousa,Amir Hossein Kargaran,Rachel Bawden,Qiaoyuan Zheng,Mateusz Lango,Beni Egressy,Fidel Rodríguez Velásquez,Natchapon Jongwiriyanurak,Minh Ngoc Do,Marco Gaido,Lena Libon,Dzmitry Kuzmin,Badal Nyalang,Antoine Taroni,Andrei Niculae,Abdulaziz Nura Kani,Rushikesh Zawar,Marek Šuppa,Beatrice Savoldi,Andreas Simons,Rayyan Merchant,Ilai Yaron Levy,Francesco Pinto,Ziyi Yang,Yolanda Xavier,Samuel Frontull,Muhammad Ravi Shulthan Habibi,Kenneth Enevoldsen,Harris Abdul Majid,Francesca Padovani,Tim Graf,Tatiana Bielakova,Sharifa Djurabaeva,Shaoxiong Ji,Raia Abu Ahmad,Pavel Stepachev,Jirui Qi,Ayush Sunil Munot,Alireza Pakniat,Ayla Rigouts Terryn,Yuxing Lu,Yurii Paniv,Xiyan Fu,Tosin Adewumi,Sunisth Kumar,Stéphane J. P. S. Thunus,Shree Harsha Bokkahalli Satish,Shayan Bali,Prakhar Gupta,Papa Abdou Karim Karou Diallo,Matija Akrap,Marko Culjak,Kristýna Onderková,Joseph Attieh,Esrael Teferi Tensay,Elisabeth Fittschen,Benoît Sagot,Jingwei Ni,Yu Fan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"最后翻译基准"（Last Translation Benchmark, LTB），这是一个持续更新的多模态（文本、图像、音频、视频）基准数据集，其中每个示例都配备了人工撰写的验证规则，用于精确描述模型在该示例上的具体失败模式，从而提供可靠且可操作的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着机器翻译模型的不断增强，现有标准基准正在趋于饱和；自动翻译指标不可靠、易受奖励黑客攻击且缺乏可操作性；甚至人工评估也常缺乏可重复性、客观性和可扩展性。这些问题阻碍了跟踪翻译领域的客观进展和识别改进方向，因此需要一个能持续挑战尖端模型并定位具体失败案例的评估方案。

Method: 提出了Last Translation Benchmark (LTB)，其核心机制包括：(1) 收集人类撰写并经同行评审的多模态示例（文本、图像、音频、视频），专门用于击垮领先的机器翻译模型；(2) 每个示例附带人工精心设计的验证规则，这些规则具体描述了该示例上发生的具体失败案例，从而实现可靠且可操作的未来评估；(3) LTB是一个实时更新的数据集，接受持续贡献；LTBv1包含2026年9月1日前被接受的贡献，未来将持续发布新版本。

Result: 发布了LTBv1，包含了2026年9月1日前被接受的所有贡献，数据涵盖文本、图像、音频和视频等多种模态，每个条目都有同行评审和对应的验证规则。

Conclusion: 通过结合人类撰写的对抗性示例与精确的验证规则，LTB提供了一种超出传统饱和指标的评估方式，使翻译评估既可靠又可操作，并可通过持续贡献保持长期有效性。

Abstract: For scientific progress, we need benchmarks that test the limits of state-of-the-art models, and evaluation methods that inform us about failure cases. As models get stronger, standard benchmarks for machine translation are approaching saturation. Further, automatic translation metrics are unreliable, vulnerable to reward-hacking, and provide unactionable assessments. Even gold human evaluation is not problem-free, because it often lacks reproducibility, objectivity, and scalability. Overall, this prevents us from tracking objective progress in the field and identifying pathways for improvement. We introduce the Last Translation Benchmark, a collection of human-authored and peer-reviewed examples (texts, images, audio, videos) that break leading machine translation models. We also present a new evaluation approach: each example comes with handcrafted verification rules describing concrete failure cases on that example, therefore allowing reliable and actionable future evaluation. The Last Translation Benchmark is a live dataset that accepts ongoing contributions. The latest version is LTBv1, containing accepted contributions prior to September 1st 2026, with future releases planned as new data is continuously collected.

</details>


### [174] [Knowledge Acquisition During Pre-training? Large Language Models Learn Better With Auxiliary Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04180)
*Joseph Lee,Yidi Huang,Dokyoon Kim,Shu Yang,Li Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过受控实验研究了大语言模型预训练中的知识获取机制，提出辅助视角（auxiliary views，即知识的重构形式）对学习有因果帮助，并系统验证了重复的必要性、token预算分配、教师模型强度、知识类型以及层间偏置与压缩等机制性表现，表明大规模语料中自然出现的知识辅助表示是预训练成功的关键因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前对LLM如何在预训练过程中获取知识仍存在理解空白。作者提出辅助视角（知识的多种重构形式）对学习具有因果性帮助，希望通过受控实验来隔离并验证这一假设，从而为"数据多样性为何重要"提供机理性解释。

Method: 设计受控实验方法：(1) 确认重复对知识获取的必要性，并阐明释义仅在较小batch size下才起作用；(2) 在固定token预算下，将token从文档重复分配至辅助视角可提升学习效果（即便对事实回忆任务也是如此，结果反直觉）；(3) 检验辅助视角的有效性是否依赖生成它的教师模型强度；(4) 识别情境性（contextual）和基础性（foundational）两类知识形态在存在先验知识缺口时的作用；(5) 通过层间偏置（layer-wise biases）和压缩（compression）分析其机制表现。

Result: (1) 重复是知识获取的必要条件，释义仅在较小batch size下有助益；(2) 在固定token预算下，将token从重复分配给辅助视角反而提升学习效果，包括事实回忆，这与此前认知相反；(3) 辅助视角的有效性不依赖于生成它的教师模型强度；(4) 识别出情境性与基础性两类知识形态在弥补先验知识缺口时能促进学习；(5) 机制层面通过层间偏置与压缩揭示了这些效应的作用方式。

Conclusion: 研究证明知识的辅助表示（auxiliary representations）——在大规模预训练语料中自然出现——是预训练成功的关键因素，并为数据多样性为何重要提供了合理的机理性解释。

Abstract: Gaps remain in our understanding of how large language models (LLMs) acquire knowledge during pre-training. We posit that auxiliary views, reformulations of knowledge, are causally helpful for learning. We design controlled experiments to isolate this. First, we confirm that repetition is necessary for acquisition and clarify that paraphrasing helps only at smaller batch sizes. Second, holding the token budget fixed, allocating tokens from document repetition to auxiliary views improves learning, counterintuitively, even for factual recall. Third, the effectiveness of auxiliary views is not contingent on the strength of the teacher model that generates them. Fourth, we identify forms of knowledge, contextual and foundational, that aid learning in the presence of prior knowledge gaps. Finally, we examine how these effects manifest mechanistically via layer-wise biases and compression. Together, our findings suggest that auxiliary representations of knowledge, which arise naturally in large pre-training corpora, are a key factor in the success of pre-training and offer a plausible explanation for why data diversity matters.

</details>


### [175] [Legibility is Not Interpretability: Comparing Judged and Actual Importance in Chain-Of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04194)
*Kevin Du,Alexander Hoyle,Laura Ruis,Acyr Locatelli*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究思维链推理步骤的文字是否真正编码了其功能重要性。作者将推理步骤的重要性操作化为优势（advantage，即包含该步骤对期望奖励如正确答案的边际变化），通过蒙特卡洛 rollout 估计，并评估LLM评分器能否识别高优势步骤。结果发现：足够强的LLM能优于发生率基线但远未达到噪声上限；将模型微调为步骤级评论家在错误回答上表现良好，但在正确回答上距上限仍有距离，表明步骤重要性仅能从推理轨迹文本中部分恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 越来越多的研究将思维链模型的推理轨迹视为理解模型推理过程的窗口，使用LLM裁判来诊断错误、评估忠实性、进行步骤级监督（如过程奖励模型和生成式评论家）。这些做法都依赖于一个隐含假设：推理步骤的文本携带着其功能角色的信息。作者希望检验这个假设是否成立——即文本是否真的编码了哪些推理步骤重要的信息。

Method: 作者将推理步骤的重要性操作化为优势（advantage）：由于包含该步骤而导致的期望奖励（如产生正确最终答案）的变化，通过蒙特卡洛 rollout 进行估计作为ground truth。然后评估LLM裁判能否识别高优势步骤，并与发生率基线和噪声上限进行比较。同时测试了将模型微调为步骤级评论家的表现。

Result: 研究发现：足够有能力的LLM裁判识别高优势步骤的表现优于发生率基线，但远低于噪声上限。将模型微调为步骤级评论家在处理不正确回答时有显著提升，但在处理正确回答时仍距离上限有较大差距。

Conclusion: 步骤重要性仅能从推理轨迹的文本中部分恢复，这提示不应将思维链的可读性视为模型的可解释性，对过程奖励建模等实践具有重要警示意义。

Abstract: Reasoning traces from chain-of-thought models appear to offer a legible window into how a model arrives at its answer. A growing body of work treats them as such, using LLM judges to diagnose errors, evaluate faithfulness, and provide step-level supervision via process reward models and generative critics. These practices rely on the text of a reasoning step carrying information about its functional role. But does the text actually encode information about which reasoning steps matter? We operationalize the importance of a reasoning step as its advantage: the change in expected reward, e.g., producing the correct final answer, from including that step, estimated via Monte Carlo rollouts. Basing ground truth on these estimates, we evaluate whether LLM judges can identify high-advantage steps and find that sufficiently capable LLMs can outperform a prevalence baseline but fall well short of a noise ceiling. Fine-tuning a model as a step-level critic yields strong improvement for incorrect responses but remains distant from ceiling for correct responses, suggesting that step importance is only partially recoverable from the text of the reasoning trace. Our findings contribute to a growing body of chain-of-thought faithfulness work that cautions against treating the legibility of reasoning traces as interpretability, especially with implications for process reward modeling.

</details>


### [176] [ESPO: Error-Structured Prompt Optimization via Diagnose, Diversify, and Stabilize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04197)
*Lihao Liu,Peng Tang,Kunwar Yashraj Singh,Shabnam Ghadar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ESPO（Error-Structured Prompt Optimization），一种针对进化式提示优化器中提示词膨胀问题的三阶段优化方法。通过诊断（结构化聚类错误）、提出（四种互补策略生成候选）、选择（bootstrap稳定性选择）三阶段分解，ESPO在七个NLP基准上平均准确率比SOTA高3.76个百分点，提示词缩短47%，并在跨模型实验中全面领先。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 进化式提示优化器（如GEPA）存在提示词膨胀问题：每次迭代都追加规则与caveats，使提示词长度可达原来的3倍但准确率并未提升。作者将其追溯至三个缺陷：不完整的错误观察（incomplete error observation）、有限的搜索多样性（limited search diversity）以及不可靠的选择（unreliable selection）。

Method: ESPO将提示优化分解为三个阶段：(1) Diagnose阶段——在单轮内将所有训练错误聚类为结构化模式；(2) Propose阶段——通过四种互补策略生成候选提示，每种策略具有独立的偏差；(3) Select阶段——应用bootstrap稳定性选择确定最终提示。泛化界（附录）表明每个阶段对应测试时差距的一个相应项。

Result: 在七个公共NLP基准（Tweet、MMLU、GSM8K、HotpotQA、ScoNe、HoVer、PUPA）上，ESPO相较于SOTA（GEPA的70.91%）将平均准确率提升3.76个百分点至74.67%，在每个数据集上均匹配或超过GEPA；同时提示词缩短47%（1004 vs 1878字符），推理更快。跨模型实验（Gemma 3 12B、Mistral 14B、Qwen3 32B、Claude Haiku 4.5）显示ESPO在每个测试模型上都取得最佳平均准确率，最大差距出现在Qwen3 GSM8K上（15.00%至91.40%）。消融实验证实：仅增加多样性而不使用bootstrap选择反而损害性能（-1.20%）。

Conclusion: ESPO通过三阶段结构化分解有效解决提示词膨胀问题，在准确率、提示词紧凑性和推理效率上全面优于现有方法。其理论泛化界为各阶段提供了严谨依据，且消融实验验证了bootstrap稳定性选择在方案中的关键作用——多样性提升必须与可靠选择机制配合才能产生正向收益。

Abstract: Evolutionary prompt optimizers such as GEPA suffer from prompt bloat: each iteration appends rules and caveats, producing prompts up to 3$\times$ longer yet no more accurate. We trace this to three deficiencies - incomplete error observation, limited search diversity, and unreliable selection - and propose ESPO (Error-Structured Prompt Optimization), which decomposes prompt optimization into three phases: Diagnose clusters all training errors into structural patterns in one round; Propose generates candidates via four complementary strategies with independent biases; Select applies bootstrap stability selection. On seven public NLP benchmarks - Tweet, MMLU, GSM8K, HotpotQA, ScoNe, HoVer, and PUPA - ESPO improves average accuracy by $+$3.76 pp over the state-of-the-art (74.67% vs 70.91% for GEPA), matching or exceeding GEPA on every dataset while producing prompts 47% shorter (1,004 vs 1,878 chars) and faster at inference. Cross-model experiments across four additional student models (Gemma 3 12B, Mistral 14B, Qwen3 32B, Claude Haiku 4.5) show ESPO yields the best average accuracy on every model tested, with the largest gap on Qwen3 GSM8K (15.00% $\to$ 91.40%). A generalization bound (Appendix) grounds each phase in a corresponding term of the test-time gap, and the ablation confirms a key prediction: adding diversity without bootstrap selection actually hurts performance ($-$1.20%).

</details>


### [177] [Compile by Training: Turning Natural-Language Specifications into Local Neural Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04199)
*Yuntian Deng,Pengyu Nie,Stuart Shieber*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出"通过训练进行编译"(compile by training)方法，将自然语言规范转化为可复用的神经函数。在编译阶段，教师模型生成任务特定示例来训练小型适配器，用于紧凑的解释器。生成的函数无需教师模型即可运行，可存储、版本化并像普通软件一样组合。在FuzzyBench-Hard基准上达到83.6%的语义准确率，虽然编译时间约一分钟，但高于快速编译器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多重复性文本函数容易用自然语言描述，但难以用规则实现。如果对每个输入都调用大型远程模型，会带来重复的成本、延迟和对服务提供商的依赖。

Method: 在编译时，使用教师模型生成任务特定的训练示例，用这些示例训练一个适用于紧凑解释器的小型适配器。编译后的函数无需教师模型即可独立运行。

Result: 在FuzzyBench-Hard（Program-as-Weights快速编译器无精确匹配的子集）上达到83.6%的语义准确率。高准确率伴随较高的编译时间成本（约一分钟，而快速编译器仅需数秒）。作者将该编译器部署为公开交互服务，并展示了多站点网站助手、语言控制的3D虚拟形象以及双向英语-Claudish翻译器等应用。

Conclusion: 该工作实现了将自然语言函数描述"编译"为持久、可复用、可组合的神经函数，在准确率上超越快速编译方法，验证了在成本与质量之间取得平衡的可行性。

Abstract: Many recurring text functions are easy to describe but difficult to implement with rules, while calling a large remote model for every input introduces repeated cost, latency, and dependency on a provider. We present compile by training, which turns a natural-language specification into a reusable neural function. At compile time, teacher models generate task-specific examples that are used to train a small adapter for a compact interpreter. The resulting function runs without the teachers and can be stored, versioned, and composed like ordinary software. On FuzzyBench-Hard, a subset on which the Program-as-Weights fast compiler produced no exact matches, compile by training reaches 83.6% semantic accuracy. This higher accuracy comes with a higher compile-time cost: roughly a minute rather than seconds for the fast compiler. We deploy the compiler in a public interactive service and demonstrate compiled functions in a multi-site website helper, a language-controlled 3D avatar, and a bidirectional English-Claudish translator.

</details>

### [178] [Markovian Shock-Source Tracing and Multidimensional Asset Roles in Exchange Rates, Gold Futures, and Bitcoin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03437)
*Seung Ho Choi,Seoin Jang,Hyunwoo Lee,Hayoung Choi*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文研究国际金融网络（主要汇率、黄金期货与比特币）中的跨资产关联性，超越传统净发送者-接收者二分法，通过直接溢出传导、平稳源追溯动态与多步上游连通性三个互补维度刻画资产角色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法仅依赖净发送者-接收者分类来刻画资产在网络中的角色，无法捕捉直接连接、源头追溯动态与多步上游可达性之间的差异，因而信息不完整。本文旨在提出一个多维度框架，以更全面地理解跨资产金融溢出结构。

Method: 使用VAR广义预测误差方差分解（VAR-GFEVD）估计回报溢出；将正成对净溢出结构映射为行随机马尔可夫核，其跃迁沿原始传导边的反方向追踪主导净溢出源；通过平稳出发通量描述该溯源链中的长期运动；采用Fink等人的确定性概率传播算法评估Viral Centrality以近似多步上游可达性。另含以美元指数为辅助变量的扩展设定。

Result: 经验上，黄金期货是主导的直接净发送者和首选平稳源追踪节点，但不具备最大的Viral Centrality；若干被归类为直接净接收者的汇率节点具有较高的Viral Centrality，表明其可广泛条件性接入上游源节点；比特币处于中间角色。含美元指数的辅助设定得到性质相似的角色分化结果。

Conclusion: 直接连通性、平稳源追踪与多步上游近似可达性无法仅从NET溢出中推断出来。所有扩散解释均为描述性的，且依赖于所估计网络、行归一化与重启闭合等设定条件。

Abstract: This study examines cross-asset connectedness in an international financial network of major exchange rates, gold futures, and Bitcoin. Moving beyond the conventional net transmitter--receiver classification, we characterize asset roles through three complementary dimensions: direct spillover transmission, stationary source-tracing dynamics, and multistep upstream connectivity. Return spillovers are estimated using VAR generalized forecast-error variance decomposition (VAR-GFEVD). The positive pairwise net-spillover structure is then mapped into a row-stochastic Markov kernel whose transitions trace dominant net-spillover sources in the reverse direction of the original transmission edges. A stationary departure flux describes long-run movement in this source-tracing chain, while Viral Centrality is evaluated by the deterministic probability-propagation algorithm of Fink et al. to approximate multistep upstream reach. Empirically, gold futures emerge as the dominant direct net transmitter and the leading stationary source-tracing node, but do not have the largest Viral Centrality. Several exchange-rate nodes classified as direct net receivers have relatively high Viral Centrality, indicating broad conditional access to upstream source nodes. Bitcoin occupies an intermediate role. An auxiliary specification including the U.S. Dollar Index yields qualitatively similar role differentiation. The results show that direct connectedness, stationary source tracing, and approximate multistep upstream reach cannot be inferred from NET spillovers alone. All diffusion interpretations are descriptive and conditional on the estimated network, row normalization, and restart closure.

</details>


### [179] [Differentiable Hybrid Modelling for Learning and Optimising Chemical Transport Processes from Experimental Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04011)
*Arthur Jessop,Mohammed Alsubeihi,Ben Moseley,Ashwin Kumar Rajagopalan*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向输运过程（特别是群体平衡方程）的通用可微混合建模框架，将JAX有限体积群体平衡求解器与可学习的神经网络组件相结合，通过真实实验数据同时用于发现本构定律和拟合初始条件，并利用框架的可微性进行过程优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统输运模型依赖人为选定的本构定律并假设初始条件已知，这两项限制会显著偏置模型预测并导致系统误差；而黑盒神经代理虽然在匹配真实数据方面更好，但局限于其训练任务且无法验证物理一致性。因此需要一种既能贴合真实数据又具备物理一致性的建模方法。

Method: 构建了一个可微混合建模框架：将JAX实现的有限体积群体平衡方程求解器与可学习的神经网络组件集成，神经网络被训练用于从真实实验数据中发现本构定律并拟合初始条件。利用整个框架的可微性，可直接对工艺参数进行梯度优化以实现过程优化目标。

Result: 该框架能够更好地对真实实验输运系统进行建模，同时利用其可微性质允许针对感兴趣的量直接优化实验设置。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了此类可微混合建模框架在学习和优化涉及质量、能量和/或动量输运的化学分离过程中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Reliable transport models are essential when modelling and optimising many chemical engineering processes, yet, most models assume hand-picked constitutive laws which may not reflect reality, and often assume initial conditions are known exactly. Both restrictions can significantly bias model predictions and lead to systematic error when used in predictive and control settings. Black-box neural surrogate alternatives for modelling can better match real example data, but are confined to the task they were trained on and cannot be interrogated for physical consistency. Here we introduce a general-purpose differentiable hybrid modelling framework for transport processes, specifically for the case of population balance equations. Our framework integrates a JAX finite volume population balance solver with learnable neural network components which are trained to both discover constitutive laws and fit initial conditions from real experimental data, allowing us to better model real experimental transport systems. Furthermore, we use our framework for process optimisation, using its differentiability to allow us to direct optimising experimental settings for quantities of interest. This work highlights the huge potential of such differentiable hybrid modelling frameworks for learning and optimising any given chemical separation which involves mass, energy, and/or momentum transport.

</details>


### [180] [Calibration of neural viscoelastic models via full-field data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03645)
*Brain M. Riemer,Markus Kästner,Karl A. Kalina*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一个用于小应变粘弹性PANN的无监督学习框架，仅需全局反作用力和表面位移等可直接从真实实验中获取的量进行训练，通过平衡间隙法、准牛顿优化器和自动微分求解约束优化问题，并采用伴随方法大幅降低训练计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理增强神经网络（PANN）用于本构建模时通常需要完整场数据或无法直接测量的量进行训练，而这些量在真实实验中难以获取。本文旨在开发一种仅依赖实验可直接测量的全场地数据的无监督标定框架，无需监督标签或其他不可直接观测的内部量。

Method: 将PANN嵌入广义标准材料理论框架，通过两个标量势函数（自由能与对偶耗散势）的不变式表示保证热力学一致性和材料对称性。针对平面应力薄试样，基于平衡间隙方法构建约束优化问题，配合准牛顿优化器和自动微分；未知离面应变由平面应力条件确定，内部变量演化由隐式时间积分方案捕获，在每个积分点和时间步通过局部牛顿迭代求解非线性方程组。为降低训练成本，采用反向伴随方法计算目标损失梯度，而非对所有牛顿迭代步骤进行反向传播。

Result: 使用合成数据（含噪声的位移和力）进行验证，结果表明该框架在广泛的变形速率和加载路径下均能取得优异的标定精度和一致性。

Conclusion: 所提出的无监督校准框架仅利用实验中可直接获取的全局力和表面位移即可有效标定小应变粘弹性PANN，在保证热力学一致性与材料对称性的同时，通过伴随方法显著降低了训练计算开销。

Abstract: We propose an unsupervised learning framework for calibrating a physics-augmented neural network (PANN) for small-strain viscoelasticity via full-field data. It only requires quantities that are directly accessible in real experiments for training, namely global reaction forces and surface displacements. The underlying PANN is embedded in the generalized standard materials theory, in which two scalar-valued potentials render the constitutive model thermodynamically consistent by construction, while invariant-based representations of the free energy and the dual dissipation potential additionally ensure material symmetry. Considering a thin specimen under the plane stress assumption, we formulate a constrained optimization problem based on the equilibrium gap method in combination with quasi-Newton optimizers and automatic differentiation. Thereby, the unknown out-of-plane strain follows from the plane stress condition and the evolution of the internal variables is captured by an implicit time integration scheme. The resulting system of nonlinear equations is solved via a local Newton iteration at quadrature point and time step. To drastically reduce the computational cost of training, the backward adjoint method is employed to compute the gradient of the target loss, instead of backpropagating through all Newton iteration steps. The proposed framework is demonstrated for synthetic data, including noisy displacements and forces, showing excellent agreement across a wide range of deformation rates and load paths.

</details>


### [181] [Influence of Extruded Filament Shape on Buildability in 3D Concrete Printing: A Geometry-Informed Deep Learning-FEM Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04028)
*Giacomo Rizzieri,Saif-Ur-Rehman,Jörg F. Unger,Annika Robens-Radermacher*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种几何感知建模框架，将基于深度学习的丝材形状预测工具ShapeGen3DCP与层激活有限元方法结合，用于评估3D混凝土打印结构在真实丝材几何形状下的可建造性。研究发现挤出参数和丝材几何形状显著影响可建造性预测，椭圆形近似是几何保真度与建模简单性之间的有效平衡，而矩形表示若需使用，基于体积守恒的尺寸定义优于按最大宽度或层间接触宽度校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D混凝土打印中沉积丝材的几何形态显著影响结构的性能与稳定性，然而现有基于有限元的可建造性评估大多将打印层简化为矩形，可能导致预测精度受限。需要开发能反映真实丝材几何形态的建模方法。

Method: 提出几何信息建模框架：集成基于深度学习的丝材形状预测器ShapeGen3DCP与层激活有限元方法。该框架直接从材料和工艺参数生成几何感知数值模型，无需实验丝材表征或高计算成本的流体流动仿真。通过实验数据验证，并对直线墙体进行参数化研究。

Result: 验证和参数化研究表明：挤出参数及丝材几何显著影响可建造性预测；自由流动沉积对真实丝材表示更敏感，而层压策略对几何简化不敏感；椭圆形近似在几何保真度和建模简单性之间效果最佳；若使用矩形表示以获得规则计算网格加速仿真，基于体积守恒确定尺寸比按最大丝材宽度或层间接触宽度校准更可靠。

Conclusion: 该研究证实了丝材几何形态对3DCP可建造性模拟的重要性，并为选择高效准确的几何表示方式提供了实用指导，有助于提升可建造性评估的预测精度与计算效率。

Abstract: The geometric morphology of deposited filaments can significantly influence the structural performance and stability of 3D concrete-printed (3DCP) structures. However, most finite element (FEM)-based approaches for buildability assessment represent printed layers as simplified rectangles, potentially limiting predictive accuracy. This study proposes a geometry-informed modelling framework that integrates the deep-learning-based filament shape prediction tool ShapeGen3DCP with a layer-activation FEM approach to investigate the effect of realistic filament geometries on buildability. The framework generates geometry-aware numerical models directly from material and process parameters, eliminating the need for experimental filament characterization or computationally intensive fluid-flow simulations. Validation against experimental data and a parametric study of rectilinear walls demonstrate that extrusion parameters and the resulting filament geometry can significantly influence buildability predictions. Realistic filament representations are particularly important for free-flow deposition, whereas layer-pressing strategies are less sensitive to geometric simplifications. Among the investigated representations, an elliptical approximation provides an effective balance between geometric fidelity and modelling simplicity. When rectangular representations are preferred to enable regular computational meshes for faster simulations, defining their dimensions based on volume conservation improves prediction reliability compared with calibrating them using either the maximum filament width or the interlayer contact width. Overall, the proposed methodology demonstrates the importance of incorporating filament geometry into 3DCP simulations and provides practical guidance for selecting efficient and accurate geometric representations for buildability assessment.

</details>


### [182] [What Do CAE Simulation Agents Really Need Beyond a Generic Harness?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03718)
*Jiasheng Shi,Tianhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文研究了在通用LLM agent框架下，CAE仿真agent还需要什么额外能力。实验表明，当信息访问和修复预算固定时，单agent框架的表现优于多agent专用系统（FoamBench 96.4% vs 88.2%），执行反馈修复是最关键的能力（将准确率从71.8%提升到96.4%），而脚本化反思没有额外收益；唯一仍然有效的外部输入是求解器教程形式提供的领域知识（从80.9%提升到96.4%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来CAE仿真agent尝试添加多agent分解、领域检索、脚本化反思等专门机制，但这些机制是针对弱基础模型设计的。现代的agent框架本身已具备多轮推理、工具使用和执行反馈能力，作者希望厘清在已有通用框架能力的条件下，CAE仿真agent还真正需要什么，以指导后续系统设计避免冗余复杂度。

Method: 作者控制信息访问和修复预算为固定因素，进行系统对比实验：将单agent通用框架与多agent专用CAE系统进行性能比较，并通过消融实验逐项去除/添加执行反馈修复、脚本化反思和领域知识（求解器教程输入），以定位各组件对最终FoamBench准确率的实际贡献。

Result: 单agent框架在FoamBench上达到96.4%准确率，优于多agent专用系统的88.2%；执行反馈修复将准确率从71.8%提升至96.4%；脚本化反思未能带来额外提升；提供求解器教程作为领域知识带来最大增益，将准确率从80.9%提升至96.4%。

Conclusion: 现代通用agent harness已充分覆盖多agent分解和脚本化反思等机制所提供的价值，CAE仿真agent只需依赖通用的执行反馈修复能力；真正稀缺且仍然有效的增强是特定求解器的领域知识（教程文档），是CAE仿真agent设计的重点投入方向。

Abstract: Computer-aided engineering (CAE) simulation is among the largest and most demanding areas of engineering, where setting up a solver such as OpenFOAM, FEniCS, or COMSOL takes real expertise. Large language model (LLM) agents promise to turn a natural-language request into a working simulation, and recent CAE agents add simulation-specific machinery: multi-agent decomposition, domain retrieval, and scripted reflection. That machinery suited weak base models; modern harnesses already supply multi-turn reasoning, tool use, and execution feedback. We ask what a CAE simulation agent still needs beyond a generic harness. With information access and repair budget held fixed, a single-agent harness matches or beats multi-agent specialized systems (FoamBench 96.4\% vs.\ 88.2\%).
  Ablations trace this to capabilities the harness already provides: execution-feedback repair lifts FoamBench from 71.8\% with no repair round to 96.4\%, while scripted reflection adds nothing. The one input that still helps is domain knowledge supplied as solver tutorials, our largest measured gain (80.9\% to 96.4\%).

</details>


### [183] [sp-DBA: a general framework for adaptive transform-domain computation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03922)
*Jingkun Jiang,Pingchuan Deng,Yang Xia*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出频谱动态块激活（sp-DBA）框架，一种针对变换域工作流的自适应加速方法。sp-DBA在计算过程中动态激活变换域计算块，将计算集中在需求之处，同时保持数值精度并优化变换操作。在材料科学、生物学和光通信等代表性工作流中，实现高达28.1倍的变换域更新加速和8.4倍的总体加速；在多达8个GPU上的强和弱扩展测试表明，该局部自适应更新在分布式FFT工作流中仍然有效。通过在不重建现有求解器的情况下将执行级自适应性引入成熟的变换域工作流，sp-DBA扩展了现代并行系统上科学模拟和信号处理计算的规模、持续时间和复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变换域方法在科学计算和信号处理中至关重要，但现有自适应策略往往引入新的数据结构或需要大量工作流重新设计，限制了在大规模并行硬件上的高效执行。

Method: 提出spectral dynamic block activation (sp-DBA)，一个自适应加速框架，在计算演化过程中动态激活变换域计算块，将计算集中在需要的位置，同时保持数值精度并优化变换操作，无需重建现有求解器即可实现执行级自适应性。

Result: 在材料科学、生物学和光通信的代表性工作流中，取得了高达28.1倍的变换域更新加速和8.4倍的总体加速；在多达8个GPU上的强扩展与弱扩展测试中，局部自适应更新在分布式FFT工作流中保持有效。

Conclusion: sp-DBA通过在不重建现有求解器的前提下为成熟变换域工作流引入执行级自适应，扩展了现代并行系统上科学模拟和信号处理计算的规模、持续时间与复杂度。

Abstract: Transform-domain methods simplify analysis and computation, making them central to scientific computing and signal processing. However, existing adaptive strategies often introduce new data structures or require substantial workflow redesign, limiting efficient execution on massively parallel hardware. Here we present spectral dynamic block activation (sp-DBA), an adaptive acceleration framework for transform-domain workflows. sp-DBA dynamically activates transform-domain computation blocks as a calculation evolves, concentrating computation where it is needed while retaining numerical accuracy and optimized transform operations. Across representative workflows in materials science, biology, and optical communications, the demonstrated implementations achieve speedups of up to 28.1-fold for transform-domain updates and up to 8.4-fold overall acceleration; strong- and weak-scaling tests on up to eight GPUs show that the local adaptive update remains effective within distributed FFT workflows. By introducing execution-level adaptivity into mature transform-domain workflows without rebuilding existing solvers, sp-DBA extends the scale, duration, and complexity of scientific simulations and signal-processing calculations on modern parallel systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [ReRoom: Blending Virtual and Physical Contexts for In Situ Room Planning in Mixed Reality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03596)
*Hongliang Yang,Yanjing Xu,Anhang Zhang,Hui Ye,Pengfei Xu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: ReRoom是一个混合现实系统，支持在真实房间内部进行原位房间布局创作。它通过虚拟房间代理与扫描房间空间配准共享布局状态，允许用户通过直接操作或语言交互来精炼布局提案，同时保留已接受的设计决策。系统采用技能引导的布局代理，将室内设计指南综合为三条原则并落实在归一化的房间表示与可复用几何检查中，在布局质量与生成效率间取得平衡。实验表明ReRoom在非矩形房间中生成高质量布局，其原位工作流相较等效的离位VR工作流提升了房间规划体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实的居家空间规划本质上是原位创作过程：用户需要在真实尺度下评估候选布局、精炼设计意图，并将已接受的决定延续到后续迭代中。然而现有方法要么将布局编辑与物理房间分离，要么对原位评估与精炼整屋方案的支持有限，缺乏能够在真实房间环境中进行连续、迭代式布局创作的系统。

Method: ReRoom通过一个与目标房间空间配准的虚拟房间代理呈现共享布局状态，使交互与布局生成始终锚定在物理环境中。用户可通过直接操作或自然语言精炼当前布局方案，已接受的位置得以保留，使每次生成的更新延续同一演进中的设计。为实现布局质量与生成效率的平衡，系统采用技能引导的布局智能代理，该代理的房间布局设计技能将综合出的室内设计指南提炼为三条原则，并将这些原则落实于扫描房间的归一化表征与可复用的几何检查中。

Result: 实验评估显示，ReRoom能够在非矩形房间中生成高质量的布局；同时，其原位工作流相比等效的离位（off-site）VR工作流显著改善了用户的房间规划体验。

Conclusion: ReRoom验证了将混合现实原位交互与技能引导的布局生成结合是提升真实房间布局创作质量与体验的有效途径，为未来智能空间规划系统提供了设计与实现参考。

Abstract: Planning a real domestic space is an in situ authoring process: users evaluate candidate layouts at true scale, refine their intent, and carry accepted decisions into later iterations. Existing approaches either separate layout editing from the physical room or provide limited support for evaluating and refining whole-room proposals in situ. We present ReRoom, a mixed-reality system for in situ room-layout authoring. ReRoom presents a shared layout state through a virtual room proxy spatially registered to the target room, allowing interaction and layout generation to remain grounded in the physical context. Users refine the current proposal through direct manipulation or language and preserve accepted placements, allowing each generated update to continue the same evolving design. To balance layout quality with generation efficiency, ReRoom uses a skill-guided layout agent whose room-layout design skill operationalizes three principles that we formulate by synthesizing established interior-design guidance for real-room layout generation. The skill grounds these principles in a normalized representation of the scanned room and reusable geometric checks. Evaluations show that ReRoom produces high-quality layouts for non-rectangular rooms, while its in situ workflow improves the room-planning experience over an otherwise equivalent off-site VR workflow. Code will be released upon acceptance of the paper.

</details>


### [185] [TileGS: Tile-Local Depth Binning for Gaussian Splatting Rasterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03613)
*Wei Tan,Matias Turkulainen,Lauri Ilola,Hamed Rezazadegan Tavakoli,Juho Kannala*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: TileGS 是一种面向3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的瓦片局部重组织方法，将每个长瓦片范围拆分为更短的深度局部范围并按前到后顺序光栅化，必要时选择性修复以匹配基准合成。在桌面和笔记本Ada GPU上，默认No-GW变体相比gsplat实现了均值1.44倍光栅内核加速（RTX 4090）及1.069x（RTX 4090）和1.094x（RTX 1000 Ada）的端到端帧加速，同时保持输出与gsplat在数值噪声级别一致（|ΔPSNR|<0.001 dB等指标）。Nsight Compute剖析表明其加速源于减少有效光栅遍历工作，而非降低字节量、改善合并、提高占用率或减少warp发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实时3D高斯泼溅虽具有高渲染质量，但标准光栅化仍遍历全局排序的瓦片流，导致每个瓦片产生过长的深度范围和沉重的几何属性内存流量，限制了光栅化性能。

Method: TileGS对高斯泼溅进行瓦片局部重组织：将每个长瓦片范围转化为一系列更短的深度局部范围，按前到后顺序光栅化这些范围，并在粗糙排序不足以匹配基线合成时采取选择性修复。通过No-GW（无几何写入）变体避免额外的几何属性写出流量。

Result: 在9场景基准测试中，默认No-GW变体在RTX 4090上实现均值1.44x光栅内核加速和1.069x端到端帧加速，在RTX 1000 Ada上实现1.094x端到端帧加速；输出与gsplat仅差数值噪声级别。Nsight剖析显示SASS线程指令总数下降1.26x，同时几何属性占剩余内存压力的85.8%（总光栅流量）和88.6%（过剩扇区）。

Conclusion: 剖析计数器支持以下解释：TileGS通过减少有效的光栅遍历工作来提升光栅性能，而非通过降低字节量、改善内存合并、提高SM占用率或直接减少测得的warp发散来实现。此外，几何属性流量成为当前系统剩余的主要内存瓶颈，为未来优化指明了方向。

Abstract: Real-time 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) achieves high rendering quality, but standard rasterization still traverses a globally sorted tile stream that creates long per-tile ranges and heavy geometry-attribute traffic. We present TileGS, a tile-local reorganization of Gaussian splatting. TileGS turns each long tile range into a sequence of shorter depth-local ranges, rasterizes those ranges in front-to-back order, and applies selective repair where coarse ordering is insufficient to match baseline compositing. Across a 9-scene benchmark on desktop and laptop Ada GPUs, our default No-GW (No Geometry-Write) variant delivers a mean 1.44x raster-kernel speedup on RTX 4090 and mean end-to-end frame speedups of 1.069x on RTX 4090 and 1.094x on RTX 1000 Ada over gsplat--a widely used optimized open-source 3DGS implementation--while matching the gsplat output up to numerical noise (|Delta PSNR| < 0.001 dB, |Delta SSIM| < 0.001, |Delta LPIPS| < 0.001). Full-suite RTX 4090 Nsight Compute profiling reveals TileGS is faster despite lower SM throughput, lower active-warp occupancy, and higher DRAM traffic, while total SASS thread instructions fall by 1.26x. Source-attributed profiling confirms that geometry attributes dominate the remaining memory pressure (85.8% of total raster traffic and 88.6% of excess sectors). Together, these counters support the interpretation that TileGS improves raster performance by reducing effective raster traversal work, rather than by reducing byte volume, improving coalescing, increasing occupancy, or directly reducing measured warp divergence.

</details>


### [186] [Reparametrizing 3D Gaussian Splatting for Real-Time Palette-based Color and Luminance Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03897)
*Cheng-Kang Ted Chao,Yotam Gingold*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对3D高斯泼溅(3DGS)的实时交互式颜色编辑框架，通过重参数化预训练vanilla 3DGS的球谐系数来编码视图相关的调色板权重，支持调色板重着色、每调色板色调曲线实现颜色感知的亮度调节，以及像素级颜色约束。编辑在数十毫秒内完成，且可高效烘焙回vanilla 3DGS保持兼容性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业的颜色编辑需要同时精确控制颜色（色相和饱和度）与亮度，且最好通过独立分离的控制实现。然而先前基于原始空间(primitive-space)的调色板3DGS方法存在alpha混合破坏单个高斯稀疏性、导致编辑向非目标区域扩散的缺陷，且无法提供每个调色板颜色的独立亮度控制。

Method: 1) 将预训练vanilla 3DGS的球谐系数重参数化，用于编码视图依赖的调色板权重而非从头训练新表示；2) 通过基于图像空间稀疏性的损失同时求解权重和调色板颜色；3) 亮度编辑实现为沿消色差轴的逐像素权重偏移，等价于逐像素的调色板感知亮度编辑；4) 采用迭代重加权最小二乘和阻尼块坐标下降协同优化色调曲线与调色板偏移；5) 结果可高效烘焙回vanilla 3DGS以保持与标准查看器兼容。

Result: 相比先前的调色板3DGS方法，该方法实现了更稀疏、更局部化的编辑，同时启用了每调色板颜色的独立亮度控制以及视图一致的像素级约束，这些能力在3DGS上此前不可用。编辑耗时仅数十毫秒，达到实时性能。

Conclusion: 提出的视图空间公式有效解决了先验原始空间方法中alpha混合破坏稀疏性导致编辑扩散的核心局限，实现了颜色与亮度的独立、分离控制，同时保持与标准vanilla 3DGS的兼容性，为3DGS提供了一套此前缺失的交互式颜色编辑能力。

Abstract: Professional color editing requires precise control over both color (hue and saturation) and lightness, ideally through separate, independent controls. We present a real-time interactive color editing framework for 3D Gaussian Splatting that supports palette-based recoloring, per-palette tone curves for color-aware luminance adjustment, and pixel-level color constraints. Rather than training a new representation from scratch, we reparameterize the spherical harmonics of a pretrained vanilla 3DGS to encode view-dependent palette weights. We simultaneously solve for weights and palette colors via a loss based on image-space sparsity. Luminance editing is realized as a per-pixel weight shift along the achromatic axis, which we show is equivalent to a per-pixel palette-aware luminance edit. This view-space formulation addresses a core limitation of prior primitive-space methods, where alpha-blending breaks per-Gaussian sparsity and causes edits to bleed into unintended regions. Our edits run in tens of milliseconds via an iteratively reweighted least squares and damped block-coordinate descent that couples tone curves and palette shifts under view-space sparsity. Our representation can be efficiently baked back into a vanilla 3DGS, preserving compatibility with standard viewers. We demonstrate sparser, more localized edits than prior palette-based 3DGS methods, while enabling independent luminance control per palette color and view-consistent pixel-level constraints, capabilities previously unavailable for 3DGS.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [187] [TRACE: Spatiotemporal Contact Memory Graph Network Simulator for Granular Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02991)
*Changjian Zhou,Negin Yousefpour,Jie Qi,Junfeng Fang,Guillermo A. Narsilio,Hans Petter Jostad*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TRACE是一种图网络模拟器，通过将交互历史直接存储在接触边上，并采用注意力消息传递和门控循环单元维护持久记忆，解决了颗粒动力学模拟中长期依赖和接触图动态变化的问题。在2D和3D颗粒柱坍缩基准上，TRACE相比现有方法将长时rolling位置误差降低31-62%、最终沉积误差降低58-89%，并获得12.2倍和8.9倍于MPM参考求解器的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习型图模拟器是高分精度求解器的高效替代方案，但颗粒运动强烈依赖粒间接触历史。当颗粒接触形成、断裂和重组时，保留这些交互历史非常困难，而现有模拟器主要将时序信息存储在节点特征或节点级记忆中，无法充分捕捉接触历史对颗粒动力学的重要性。

Method: TRACE将交互历史直接存储在接触边上。每条边通过注意力机制的消息传递和门控循环单元维护持久记忆，并利用边身份字典在接触图变化时保留这些记忆。物理结构化解码器预测粒间法向和切向接触力，施加库仑摩擦极限，并应用等大反向内力。训练采用单步预训练加自回归滚动微调的两阶段策略。

Result: 在2D和3D颗粒柱坍缩基准上，TRACE产生了稳定且物理一致的长时间滚动预测，准确复现了最终沉积几何和坍缩中释放的动能。相比GNS和NMGNS，TRACE将长时滚动位置误差降低31-62%，最终沉积误差降低58-89%，同时使用更少参数并保持近乎零的颗粒互穿透。此外，TRACE在2D和3D中分别比MPM参考求解器快12.2倍和8.9倍。

Conclusion: 将交互历史直接存储在接触边上的边级记忆机制能更有效地捕获颗粒动力学中关键的接触历史依赖，相比节点级记忆方案在精度、参数效率和物理一致性方面均有显著优势，为学习型颗粒模拟器提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Learned graph simulators provide an efficient alternative to high-fidelity solvers for granular dynamics. However, granular motion depends strongly on inter-granular contact history, which is difficult to preserve when particle contacts form, break, and rearrange. Existing simulators mainly store temporal information in node features or node-level memory. Here we introduce TRACE, a graph-network simulator that stores interaction history directly on contact edges. Each edge maintains a persistent memory updated by attention-based message passing and a gated recurrent unit, while an edge-identity dictionary preserves this memory as the contact graph changes. A physics-structured decoder predicts inter-granular normal and tangential contact forces, enforces the Coulomb friction limit, and applies equal-and-opposite internal forces. The model is trained with single-step pretraining followed by autoregressive rollout fine-tuning. We evaluate TRACE on 2D and 3D granular column-collapse benchmarks. In both cases, TRACE produces stable, physically consistent long-horizon rollouts, closely reproducing the final deposit geometry and the kinetic energy released during collapse. Compared with graph network simulator (GNS) and node-memory graph neural simulator (NMGNS), TRACE reduces long-rollout position error by 31-62% and final-deposit error by 58-89% across the two benchmarks, while using fewer parameters and maintaining near-zero particle interpenetration. TRACE also achieves 12.2$\times$ and 8.9$\times$ speedups over the material point method (MPM) reference solver in 2D and 3D, respectively. Our code is available at https://github.com/Data-Driven-Computational-Geotechnics/TRACE.

</details>


### [188] [The Geometry of Ignorance: LLMs Know When to Temper Bayesian Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02959)
*Toni J. B. Liu,Jiajun Bao,Yizhou Liu,Gurbir Arora,Nicolas Boullé,Raphaël Sarfati,Christopher J. Earls*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现语言模型在信息不足时会回退到一种"无知方向"——即unembedding矩阵中编码了训练语料unigram分布的单方向，作为模型不确定时的贝叶斯先验。该方向在所有四种模型家族（Llama、Qwen、Gemma、Pythia，0.4B至405B参数）中均存在。将最终预测状态投影到此方向上得到先验载荷因子λ，λ随上下文信息量增加而稳定下降。该投影将预测状态分解为两个正交向量，恰好对应一个温度调节的贝叶斯更新中的两个因子（unigram先验和上下文似然），从而校准λ使其在模型规模和家族间可比。最后证明该方向具有因果活性：调整λ可沿KL散度将预测推向或远离unigram先验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索语言模型在缺乏上下文线索时做出预测的机制——模型性究竟是依赖训练语料的统计先验还是上下文的语义信息，以及这一先验如何在几何上编码和发挥作用。

Method: 通过分析unembedding矩阵的几何结构识别出编码unigram分布的单方向（"无知方向"）；定义先验载荷因子λ为最终预测状态在该方向上的投影；形式化地将投影分解为正交的先验项和似然项，对应温度调节的贝叶斯更新；并通过因果干预实验（抬高/降低λ）验证该方向的因果作用。

Result: 在四种模型家族及0.4B至405B的参数范围内均观察到"无知方向"的存在；λ随上下文信息增加而递减；该几何分解与温度化贝叶斯更新的数学形式精确对应；大模型在高上下文情境下往往表现出更低的先验依赖；因果干预表明调整λ可以按KL散度方向控制预测偏向先验的程度。

Conclusion: 语言模型的不可知预测行为可以被解释为其unembedding几何中编码的语料unigram先验作为贝叶斯回退机制的结果，且这一机制在不同规模和架构的模型中普遍存在并因果可控，为理解模型不确定性处理提供了几何-概率框架。

Abstract: What does a language model predict when it has few clues? The answer lurks in its unembedding geometry: a single direction of the unembedding matrix encodes the unigram distribution of the training corpus, which serves as the Bayesian prior the model falls back on when uncertain. This structure --- which we term the \emph{direction of ignorance} --- appears in all four model families examined (\texttt{Llama}, \texttt{Qwen}, \texttt{Gemma}, and \texttt{Pythia}), ranging from 0.4B to 405B parameters. Projecting the final prediction state onto this direction yields a per-token \emph{prior loading factor} $λ$, which, empirically, declines steadily as the context becomes more informative. Formally, the same projection decomposes the prediction state into two orthogonal vectors that correspond exactly to the two factors of a tempered Bayesian update: a unigram prior raised to the exponent $λ$ and a context-driven likelihood. This geometric-probabilistic interpretation calibrates $λ$, making it meaningfully comparable across model sizes and families, with larger models generally exhibiting lower prior reliance in the high-context limit. Finally, we show that the direction of ignorance is causally active: raising or lowering $λ$ at the final prediction state steers the prediction toward or away from the unigram prior in KL divergence.

</details>


### [189] [Equation Recast for Canonical Operator Learning Across Parametric PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02982)
*Qiyun Cheng,Valentin Duruisseaux,Cesar F. Clauser,Md Hossain Sahadath,Huihua Yang,Shaowu Pan,Nathaniel Ferraro,Anima Anandkumar,Wei Ji,Cristina Rea*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"equation recast"（方程重构）方法，将参数化的算子学习重构为学习单一的正则算子，通过从控制方程中解析地导出参数诱导的算子变化并将其吸收到有效源项中，实现在新参数域上的零样本预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在广泛的参数范围内学习求解算子需要大量覆盖输入函数和物理参数的数据，尤其是纯数据驱动的参数化模型。此外，这类模型可能在训练分布之外静默失效，缺乏可靠性保障。

Method: 将参数化算子学习重新表述为单个正则算子的学习。参数引起的算子变化从控制方程中解析推导，并吸收进有效源项（effective sources）中，从而支持跨新参数域的零样本预测。同时利用收敛损失作为重构迭代失败的内在警告信号。

Result: 在多参数、非线性和奇异PDE设置中实现了外推；能够以共享正则表示整合稀疏异构数据集；在高保真托卡马克核聚变模拟中，通过正则域映射统一了四个设备几何结构的电子温度数据，并成功训练于单一联合算子。

Conclusion: 方程重构方法为可复用的神经PDE求解器提供了路径，兼具方程引导的迁移能力、数据效率和可监控的推理过程。

Abstract: Learning solution operators across broad parameter ranges can require substantial coverage of both input functions and physical parameters, particularly for purely data-driven parametric models. In addition, the resulting models may fail silently outside the training distribution. We introduce equation recast, which reformulates parametric operator learning as the learning of a single canonical operator. Parameter-induced operator variations are derived analytically from the governing equation and absorbed into effective sources, enabling zero-shot prediction across new parameter regimes. Across multi-parameter, nonlinear, and singular PDE settings, equation recast supports extrapolation, integrates sparse heterogeneous datasets in a shared canonical representation, and uses loss of convergence as an internal warning signal for failure of the recast iteration. In high-fidelity tokamak simulations for nuclear fusion, the framework unifies electron-temperature data across four device geometries through canonical-domain mapping within one jointly trained operator. Equation recast provides a route toward reusable neural PDE solvers combining equation-guided transfer, data efficiency, and monitorable inference.

</details>


### [190] [From Euclidean to Graph-Structured Data: A Survey of Collaborative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02984)
*Rémi Bourgerie,Šarūnas Girdzijauskas,Viktoria Fodor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于从欧几里得数据到图结构数据的协作学习综述，梳理了联邦学习与去中心化学习的基本原理、图分布场景的分类学、统计异质性以及标准化问题建模与算法框架，并指出了开放挑战与未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器学习（集中式数据收集、训练与推理）面临可扩展性和隐私等根本性限制。协作学习（联邦学习、去中心化学习）虽已提出但大多局限于欧几里得数据（如图像、文本），未能充分处理众多真实应用中由图表示的关系型结构数据。图学习固有的消息传递机制与协作环境在概念上高度契合，但图结构化数据在协作场景中的机遇与挑战仍未被充分探索，亟需系统性的整合与梳理。

Method: 本文采用综述性研究方法：首先回顾欧几里得数据上协作学习的基础原理，按学习有效性、效率与隐私保护三个核心维度进行组织；随后将讨论扩展至图结构化数据，提出图分布场景的分类学，刻画相关的统计异质性，并建立标准化的问题表述与算法框架；最后系统性识别开放挑战和前景研究方向。

Result: 文章提出了一套涵盖欧几里得与图数据两大领域的协作学习综合框架：建立了三维度（有效性、效率、隐私）分析视角；针对图数据引入了图分布场景分类体系并明确了各类统计异质性；构建了标准化问题建模与算法框架，为协作图学习提供了系统化参考。

Conclusion: 协作学习从欧几里得数据向图结构化数据的扩展是一个新兴且极具潜力的研究方向。尽管已取得初步进展，该领域仍面临诸多未解决的开放问题，需要未来研究在算法设计、理论保证与实际应用等方面持续推进。

Abstract: The conventional approach to machine learning, that is, collecting data, training models, and performing inference in a single location, faces fundamental limitations, including scalability and privacy, that restrict its applicability. To address these challenges, recent research has explored collaborative learning approaches, including federated learning and decentralized learning, where individual agents perform training and inference locally, with limited collaboration. Most collaborative learning research focuses on Euclidean data with regular, grid-like structure (e.g., images, text). However, these approaches fail to capture the relational patterns in many real-world applications, best represented by graphs. Learning on graphs relies on message-passing mechanisms to propagate information between connected nodes, making it conceptually well-suited for collaborative environments where agents must exchange information. Yet, the opportunities and challenges of learning on graph-structured data in collaborative settings remain largely underexplored. This survey provides a comprehensive investigation of collaborative learning from Euclidean to graph-structured data, aiming to consolidate this emerging field. We begin by reviewing its foundational principles for Euclidean data, organizing them along three core dimensions: learning effectiveness, efficiency, and privacy preservation. We then extend the discussion to graph-structured data, introducing a taxonomy of graph distribution scenarios, characterizing associated statistical heterogeneities, and developing standardized problem formulations and algorithmic frameworks. Finally, we systematically identify open challenges and promising research directions.

</details>


### [191] [Constant regret in general games via higher-order optimism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04113)
*Omar Abbadi,Rida Laraki,Panayotis Mertikopoulos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种非耦合学习算法HOOD（高阶乐观与折扣），当所有玩家在任意N玩家、每人至多K个动作的正则型博弈中使用时，可在整个博弈时域上保证O(N³log²K)的个人遗憾，这是一个依赖N的多项式且关于时间均匀一致的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在一般博弈中实现常数（随时间无关的）遗憾是一个关键挑战。此前方法的主要障碍是无法以受控方式抑制博弈序列中产生的大幅振荡，因此需要一种新机制来消除这一障碍，从而实现常数遗憾界。

Method: HOOD是乐观跟随正则化领导者（OptFTRL）的一个变体，其关键创新在于：1）使用折扣的(N+1)阶预测器；2）在博弈策略空间的适当"提升"（lifting）上使用熵正则化。这两种成分的组合被特意设计为以受控方式抑制诱导的博弈序列的大幅振荡。

Result: 该算法保证了O(N³log²K)的个体遗憾界，该界在博弈时域上均匀一致（即与时间T无关的常数界）。文中还指出该工作与Liu、Farina和Ozdaglar的独立并发工作有显著相似性，后者通过高阶乐观和指数移动平均估计器获得了O(N²¹log⁴K)的遗憾界。

Conclusion: HOOD通过高阶乐观预测与折扣技术的结合，成功打破了此前一般博弈中常数遗憾实现的障碍，取得了显著优于并发工作的遗憾界（O(N³log²K)对比O(N²¹log⁴K)）。

Abstract: We introduce an uncoupled learning algorithm which, when employed by all players of an arbitrary $N$-player normal form game with up to $K$ actions per player, guarantees $O(N^3\log^2 K)$ individual regret, uniformly over the horizon of play. The proposed algorithm - which we call higher-order optimism with discounting (HOOD) is a variant of optimistic follow-the-regularized-leader (OptFTRL) that combines a discounted $(N+1)$-th order predictor with entropic regularization over a suitable "lifting" of the game's strategy space. This combination of ingredients is purposefully designed to dampen large oscillations of the induced sequence of play in a controlled manner, removing in this way a key stumbling block of previous attempts to achieve constant regret in general games. Our approach bears several striking similarities to the concurrent - and completely independent - work of Liu, Farina, and Ozdaglar (arXiv:2608.31166), who very recently derived an $O(N^{21}\log^{4} K)$ regret bound through the use of higher-order optimism and an exponential moving average estimator.

</details>


### [192] [Modern Transformers Are Implicit Hybrids: From Functional Differentiation to Principled Hybrid Architecture Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02986)
*Runlin Shi,Bojian Yin,Guoqi Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出两种干预度量指标（RFIS和RPD），基于RoPE Transformer头部级功能组织，验证了检索头与位置头存在完整分类学，并提出"全局位置带"(GPBand)概念。据此提出两条原则并实例化为HwH（头部级混合架构），以不足1:3的FA/LA比例实现强语言建模与零样本长上下文外推能力的大幅提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的全注意力(FA)与线性注意力(LA)混合架构在头部分配上依赖启发式规则，缺少证据支撑。研究者希望从RoPE Transformer学习到的头部级功能组织中寻找有依据的分配基础，以指导更合理的混合架构设计。

Method: 提出两种干预度量指标：RFIS（度量每个频域对头部注意力分布的影响）和RPD（分离对旋转位置调制的依赖）。在Qwen3系列和Llama3.1上验证检索头与位置头的完整分类学；通过受控Transformer实验确认这一边界随训练长度位置尺度而变化，命名为GPBand。基于此提出两条设计原则，并构建HwH架构：NoPE全注意力用于全局检索，线性注意力用于本地位置建模，在头部粒度上按层分配功能。

Result: 在FA与LA比例低于1:3的情况下，HwH在保持强语言建模和常识推理能力的同时，相较Transformer、LA及层级混合基线提升了检索能力，并大幅增强了零样本长上下文外推性能。消融实验验证了两条原则及各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 分析揭示了零样本长度外推失败的潜在成因（GPBand与训练长度位置尺度的关系），证明了有原则的（principled）混合架构设计是通向未来基础模型的有前景路径，优于依赖启发的层或头部级分配。

Abstract: Hybrid architectures combining Full Attention (FA) and Linear Attention (LA) are increasingly prominent, yet their allocation remains heuristic. We seek an evidence-grounded basis in head-level functional organization learned by RoPE-based Transformers. Behavioral probes do not yield a complete taxonomy, so we propose two intervention metrics: RoPE Frequency Importance Score (RFIS), measuring how each frequency affects a head's attention distribution, and RoPE Positional Dependence (RPD), isolating dependence on rotary positional modulation. On Qwen3-series models and Llama3.1, RFIS suggests and RPD verifies a complete taxonomy of retrieval and positional heads separated by a salient mid-low-frequency band. Controlled Transformers show that this boundary follows the training-length positional scale; we term it the Global Positional Band (GPBand). The analysis suggests a potential cause of zero-shot length-extrapolation failure and yields two principles: positional modeling should operate only locally, with global access through position-independent retrieval; and both functions should be assigned at head granularity with layer-specific allocation. We instantiate them in Head-wise Hybrid Architecture (HwH), using NoPE FA for global retrieval and LA for local positional modeling. With an FA-to-LA ratio below 1:3, HwH retains strong language modeling and commonsense reasoning while improving retrieval and substantially strengthening zero-shot long-context extrapolation over Transformer, LA, and a layer-wise hybrid baseline. Ablations validate both principles and component roles, highlighting principled hybrid architecture design as a promising route toward future foundation models.

</details>


### [193] [Robust PAC Learning of Concurrent Stochastic Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04189)
*Angel Y. He,David Parker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了通用和随机博弈（CSG）在转移不确定性下首个PAC学习框架，能够计算社会福利最优的ε-近似纳什均衡（NE），或对不存在精确NE的情况给出可靠证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有转移不确定性的通用和随机博弈中，纳什均衡的存在性本身就是一个挑战性问题。现有强化学习方法通常假设NE必然存在或忽视不确定性，缺乏同时对均衡存在性进行推理和学习的统一框架。

Method: 算法维护基于数据的转移核L1置信集，通过求解鲁棒CSG来计算社会福利最优的ε-NE；采用基于鲁棒MDP的探索机制驱动联合状态-动作覆盖；引入Nash margin刻画，使框架要么返回ε近似NE，要么输出精确NE不存在的可靠证明。

Result: 在最小可达性条件p_reach>0下，算法在多项式个轨迹样本后终止，样本复杂度为Õ(R_max²H⁴|S|²|A|/(p_reach·ε²))。基准CSG上的实验展示了接近最优的性能、对均衡（不）存在性的正确处理以及与理论一致的样本复杂度。

Conclusion: 该工作给出了第一个能够同时处理通用和随机博弈中均衡学习与均衡存在性证明问题的PAC框架，通过Nash margin机制从理论上弥合了不确定环境下NE存在性与学习算法的鸿沟。

Abstract: We introduce the first Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) learning framework for general-sum concurrent stochastic games (CSGs) with transition uncertainty, while addressing the challenge of Nash equilibrium (NE) existence. Our algorithm maintains data-driven $L^1$ confidence sets over transition kernels and solves a robust CSG to compute a social-welfare optimal $\varepsilon$-NE, using a robust MDP-based exploration mechanism to drive joint state-action coverage. Crucially, we introduce a Nash margin characterisation that enables principled reasoning about equilibrium existence: the framework either returns an $\varepsilon$-approximate NE whose social-welfare value is $\varepsilon$-close to optimal, or provides a sound certificate that no exact NE exists. Under a minimum reachability condition $p_{\mathrm{reach}}>0$ over relevant state-action pairs, the algorithm terminates after a polynomial number of trajectory samples, with sample complexity $\widetilde{O}\left( {R_{\max}^2 H^4 |S|^2 |A| / (p_{\mathrm{reach}} \varepsilon^2)} \right)$. Empirical results on benchmark CSGs demonstrate near-optimal performance, correct handling of equilibrium (non-)existence, and sample complexity consistent with theory.

</details>


### [194] [Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02987)
*Shrinivas Ramasubramanian,Daman Arora,Fahim Tajwar,Guanning Zeng,Qingyang Wu,Zhongzhu Zhou,Chenfeng Xu,Haiwen Feng,Yuda Song,Aarti Singh,Ruslan Salakhutdinov,J. Andrew Bagnell,Jeff Schneider,Andrea Zanette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出TailRL方法，通过最大化超过随机选择奖励阈值的对数概率，直接优化策略在高奖励尾部结果的覆盖概率，而非仅优化平均奖励。只需修改优势函数即可兼容现有RL管线，在多个任务上验证了利用罕见高奖励样本提升训练与推理采样收益的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统强化学习优化平均奖励，但对生成式策略而言，两个策略可能拥有相同的平均奖励却在产出罕见高奖励结果（rollout）的概率上差异显著。随着训练和推理阶段采样增多，保留高奖励结果的概率质量变得至关重要。平均奖励指标掩盖了这一关键差异，因此需要直接优化这种覆盖度（coverage）。

Method: 不再只考虑期望奖励，而是考虑所有上尾（upper tails）：对每个奖励阈值评估策略超过该阈值的概率，将连续奖励转化为一族二元成功事件。由此提出Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning (TailRL)，最大化超过随机选取奖励阈值的对数概率。其梯度对罕见高奖励rollout赋予更大权重，可解释为Best-of-k梯度的混合。TailRL仅需对优势函数做简单修改，即可无缝兼容现有RL管线。

Result: 在目标定位、迷宫导航、GUI接地和代码优化等多项任务中，TailRL能够利用罕见的高奖励训练样本避免陷入次优解，并在推理阶段增加采样时获得比基线更大的性能提升，表明其模型更好地保留了高奖励结果的概率质量。

Conclusion: TailRL通过聚焦奖励分布的上尾覆盖而非平均指标，为生成式策略提供了一种更有利于利用采样规模优势的优化目标。该方法改动极小、兼容性高，但能在多项任务中带来显著的鲁棒性与推理期性能增益。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning typically optimizes average reward. For generative policies, the average can hide an important distinction: two policies can achieve the same mean reward while having very different chances of producing a rare but high-reward rollout. This matters as sampling increases during training and inference, since its benefit depends on retaining probability mass on high-reward outcomes. We propose to optimize this coverage directly. Rather than considering only expected reward, we consider all of its upper tails: for each reward threshold, how likely is the policy to exceed it? This turns a continuous reward into a family of binary success events. We introduce Tail-Likelihood Reinforcement Learning (TailRL), which maximizes the log-probability of exceeding a randomly chosen reward threshold. Its gradient gives more weight to rare, high-reward rollouts and can be interpreted as a mixture of Best-of-(k) gradients. TailRL requires only a simple modification to the advantage function, making it compatible with existing reinforcement learning pipelines. Across object localization, maze navigation, GUI grounding, and code optimization, TailRL leverages rare high-reward training samples to avoid suboptimal solutions and yields models that benefit more from additional samples at inference time.

</details>


### [195] [Mesh-Native Physics-Informed Graph Surrogates for TCAD-in-the-Loop Design Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02988)
*Leonid Popryho,Ayoub Sadeghi,Inna Partin-Vaisband*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种物理信息图注意力网络（GAT）代理模型，直接在四面体TCAD网格上预测漂移-扩散系统的静电势与电子/空穴准费米能级，结合有限体积电流连续性残差嵌入物理约束。该模型具备尺寸泛化能力（少鳍训练的模型可直接用于大阵列），并通过深度集成的逐节点不确定性驱动主动学习筛选设计。在Sentry设备基准上以远高于全仿真器的吞吐量复现三场分布，可用于跨设备尺度的大规模Pareto前沿探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真TCAD漂移-扩散仿真计算开销大，尤其是3D结构运行时随网格复杂度急剧上升，严重制约多目标设计空间探索。现有机器学习代理仅将固定设计参数映射到少量标量指标，丢弃底层物理，缺乏跨几何与器件族的迁移性。

Method: 提出物理信息图注意力网络（GAT）代理：(1) 直接在四面体TCAD网格上操作，将网格视为图；(2) 预测每个网格节点的静电势和电子/空穴准费米能级（漂移-扩散系统的三个基本未知量）；(3) 训练损失由数据损失与基于有限体积法的电流连续性残差组成，将载流子输运物理嵌入目标；(4) 通过深度集成提供逐节点不确定性，驱动主动学习循环快速筛选大规模候选设计池，仅将信息量最大的设计送交全仿真。

Result: 在Sentaurus Device多鳍三栅FinFET基准测试中，代理以低于每场1V的RMSE准确复现三个漂移-扩散场，每个设计的吞吐量比全仿真器高数个数量级。优势随器件尺寸增大而增强：对直接仿真过慢的大型多鳍阵列，推理仍可在每器件亚秒内完成，实现直接TCAD扫描不可行的跨器件尺度Pareto前沿探索。

Conclusion: 物理信息GAT代理有效解决了TCAD仿真的计算瓶颈，兼具物理保真度、尺寸泛化性与高吞吐量，实现了此前不可行的跨器件尺度设计空间多目标探索。网格级操作保留了底层物理信息并支持跨几何迁移，主动学习进一步提升了样本效率。

Abstract: High-fidelity TCAD simulation of drift-diffusion transport remains the workhorse of emerging FinFET device design, but it is computationally expensive, especially for 3D structures where runtime escalates steeply with mesh complexity. This sharply limits multi-objective design space exploration. Existing machine-learning surrogates map a fixed set of design parameters to a few scalar device metrics, discarding the underlying physics and losing transferability across device geometries and families. A physics-informed graph attention network (GAT) surrogate is proposed. It operates directly on the tetrahedral TCAD mesh and predicts, at every mesh node, the electrostatic potential together with the electron and hole quasi-Fermi levels, the fundamental unknowns of the drift-diffusion system. Training combines a data loss with finite-volume current-continuity residuals, embedding carrier-transport physics into the objective. Operating on the mesh as a graph, the surrogate inherits size generalization: a model trained on few-fin meshes applies unchanged to substantially larger arrays, bounded at inference only by GPU memory. Per-node uncertainty from a deep ensemble drives an active-learning loop that screens large candidate pools in seconds and forwards only the most informative designs for full simulation. Benchmarked against Sentaurus Device on multi-fin tri-gate FinFETs, the surrogate reproduces the three drift-diffusion fields with sub-volt per-field RMSE and reaches a per-design throughput orders of magnitude higher than the full simulator. The advantage grows with device size: on large multi-fin arrays that are prohibitively slow to simulate directly, inference still completes in under a second per device, enabling Pareto-front exploration across device scales infeasible for direct TCAD sweeps.

</details>


### [196] [No-Regret Bayesian Optimization with Finite-Library Input-Warped Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02993)
*Edvin Ketabati Augustinsson,Robert A. Bridges*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FLIWBO（有限库输入扭曲贝叶斯优化），从有限的平滑输入映射库中选择扭曲函数以适配输入几何结构，在保留GP-UCB的高概率收敛保证的同时加速学习，并在高维多智能体系统设计与超参数优化等基准上显著优于原始坐标GP-UCB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GP-BO在代价高昂的黑箱函数优化中表现卓越，但GP-UCB的收敛性证明要求固定核函数。然而当原始坐标与目标函数的输入-输出几何不匹配时（如对数尺度超参数、局部尖峰），固定核会严重损害采样效率。输入扭曲虽能大幅改善采样效率，但现有GP-UCB理论依赖于固定核，缺乏带保证的自适应扭曲方法。

Method: FLIWBO从有限的平滑输入映射库中按任意历史相关规则选取扭曲映射，库大小引起O(√N_ε)的显式代价。在温和假设下，该方法在自适应输入几何的同时保持高概率收敛保证，并给出库大小代价的理论刻画。

Result: 受控诊断实验表明，有限库扭曲能修复人为设置的几何失配并识别出FLIWBO的失效情形。在扭曲合成目标、置信围栏陷阱、Fashion-MNIST HPO等四个重复基准上，FLIWBO-UCB在几何失配下优于原始坐标GP-UCB，能逃脱甚至击败oracle-warp期望改进的陷阱，恢复手工对数尺度的大部分收益，且在支持匹配遗憾保证的方法中领先。20维MAS设计研究证明了在高代价噪声评估下的可行性。

Conclusion: FLIWBO弥合了输入扭曲的实践收益与GP-UCB收敛理论之间的鸿沟，在任意历史相关规则下都能保持高概率收敛保证，为几何失配的黑箱优化提供了一种兼具理论保证与实践有效性的通用框架。

Abstract: Gaussian-process Bayesian optimization (GP-BO) excels at black-box optimization of costly functions, e.g., hyperparameter optimization (HPO) and multi-agent system (MAS) design. Convergence-rate guarantees exist for select methods, notably GP upper confidence bound (GP-UCB), but require a fixed kernel. Critically, the kernel encodes how input proximity affects objective value similarity. When raw coordinates poorly match this geometry - as with log-scaled hyperparameters or localized peaks - input warping can greatly improve sample efficiency, yet known GP-UCB proofs require a fixed kernel. We propose Finite-Library Input-Warped Bayesian Optimization (FLIWBO), which selects warps from a finite library of smooth input maps by any history-dependent rule. It adapts the input geometry to accelerate learning while retaining high-probability convergence guarantees under mild hypotheses, with an explicit $\sqrt(N_\varepsilon)$ library-size cost. Controlled diagnostics show that finite-library warping repairs planted geometry mismatches and identify FLIWBO failure cases. Across four repeated benchmarks - warped synthetic objectives, a confidence-fence trap, and Fashion-MNIST HPO - FLIWBO-UCB beats raw-coordinate GP-UCB under misspecified geometry, escapes traps that defeat even oracle-warp expected improvement, and recovers much of the gain from manual log scaling, while leading the tested methods that admit a matching regret guarantee. A 20-dimensional MAS design study further shows feasibility under costly noisy evaluations. Code for experiments is available: https://github.com/edvin-ketabati/bogp-paper-experiments.

</details>


### [197] [Evaluating Graph Neural Networks for Change-Criticality Classification in Maritime Navigation Charts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02996)
*Abhishek Potnis,Jacob Arndt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将电子海图（ENC）空间矢量数据集表示为图结构，将旧版与新版ENC数据集编码为图对，采用图神经网络（GNN）进行图对分类任务，以判断ENC更新对航行安全构成的是关键性还是非关键性风险，实验结果验证了基于图的表示方法能有效改进ENC更新的分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 维护ENC数据集具有挑战性，根据对象变更对航行安全的重要性进行分类尤为关键。然而目前尚不清楚何种消息传递操作、架构配置和图表示最适合对ENC中对象变化进行分类，因此需要探索基于GNN的方法来自动化或改进该维护流程。

Method: 将ENC矢量导航数据集表示为图结构，其中空间对象作为节点，空间与语义关系形成边。分别将旧版和新版ENC数据集编码为一对图，将任务形式化为图对分类问题。在此表示基础上，训练并评估多种GNN架构和模型配置，对海事专家审阅的ENC变更数据进行关键/非关键航行安全风险分类。

Result: 实验结果表明，基于图的表示方法显著改善了ENC更新的分类性能，提供了一种可扩展的方案来自动化或改进ENC维护工作流程。

Conclusion: 图神经网络结合图对的表示框架在ENC更新分类任务中有效可行，为海事领域电子导航图数据维护提供了一种可扩展的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Graph neural networks (GNNs) are a class of neural networks suitable for learning on graph-structured data. Their application to spatial data is a natural extension, however its relatively unclear which message-passing operations, architectural configurations, and graph representation is best suited for classifying changes to objects in electronic navigational charts (ENCs)--geospatial vector datasets used for marine navigation. Maintaining these datasets is a challenge, and categorizing changes to objects in the ENC based on their significance to navigational safety is of particular importance. Here, we propose to represent these vector navigation datasets as a graph structure where the spatial objects serve as nodes and their spatial and semantic relationships form edges. We encode both the old ENC dataset and new ENC dataset into a pair of graphs and frame the task as a graph-pair classification problem. Building on this representation, we investigate the use of GNN architectures to classify whether the encoded graphs constitutes a critical or non-critical risk to navigational safety. We train and evaluate several GNN architectures and model configurations on ENC changes reviewed by maritime experts. Our results demonstrate that graph-based representations improve the classification of ENC updates, providing a scalable approach for automating or improving ENC maintenance workflows.

</details>


### [198] [Verify Before You Distill: Prompt-Level Teacher Gating for On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02998)
*Zhiwei Zhang,Zechen Sun,Fei Zhao,Kang Peng,Bin Liang,Huayu Deng,Yao Hu,Kam-Fai Wong,Mu Chuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TGOPD（Teacher-Gated On-Policy Distillation），一种在提示（prompt）层面验证教师模型可靠性后再决定是否引入密集监督的蒸馏方法，在数学、代码、指令跟随等多领域及不同规模学生模型上优于Vanilla OPD，同时大幅提升异步OPD中教师节点GPU利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Vanilla OPD对每个提示均匀施加来自冻结教师的密集token级监督，未检查教师在特定提示上是否可靠。由于反向KL具有mode-seeking特性，错误但自信的教师会产生强而误导的更新。已有的熵或师生似然一致性等分布性代理只能度量不确定性或一致性，不能直接验证输出的正确性，因此需要一种在提示层面直接验证教师可靠性的机制。

Method: TGOPD在提示层面验证教师可靠性：使用少量由验证器（verifier）评分的教师探测样本估计可靠性；当可靠性检查通过时，该提示专属地走密集OPD路径；当检查未通过时，则改用基于验证器的GRPO。在异步OPD中，利用原本空闲的教师容量进行可靠性估计。

Result: 在4B和35B学生模型上，覆盖数学、代码和指令跟随任务，TGOPD在全部六个单领域设置中均优于Vanilla OPD；在多领域训练下，两个规模上的七基准平均分均更高。另外，在测得的4B单领域运行中，教师节点GPU利用率从9.8%提升至78.9%。

Conclusion: 在提示层面验证教师可靠性是改进OPD的有效原则：既能提升多领域、多规模下的蒸馏性能，又能复用空闲教师计算资源进行可靠性估计，减少异步OPD中的教师侧算力浪费，实现性能与资源利用率的双重提升。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) accelerates post-training by providing dense token-level supervision from a frozen teacher on the student's own rollouts. Vanilla OPD applies this supervision uniformly across prompts, without checking whether the teacher is reliable for each prompt. Because reverse KL is mode-seeking, a confidently wrong teacher can induce a strong yet misleading update. Distributional proxies, such as entropy or teacher-student likelihood agreement, measure uncertainty or agreement but do not directly verify outcome correctness. We introduce Teacher-Gated On-Policy Distillation (TGOPD), built on the principle that teacher reliability should be verified at the prompt level before dense supervision is admitted. TGOPD estimates reliability from a small set of verifier-scored teacher probes and routes each prompt exclusively to dense OPD when the reliability check passes or to verifier-grounded GRPO otherwise. Across 4B and 35B students in mathematics, code, and instruction following, TGOPD outperforms Vanilla OPD in all six single-domain settings and achieves higher seven-benchmark averages at both scales under multi-domain training. By using otherwise-idle teacher capacity for reliability estimation, TGOPD also reduces teacher-side compute waste in asynchronous OPD, increasing teacher-node GPU utilization from 9.8% to 78.9% in the measured 4B single-domain run.

</details>


### [199] [Causal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03003)
*Christopher Stith,Hossein Rahmani,Jesse C. Cresswell*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对新兴的"因果基础模型"(Causal Foundation Models, CFMs)进行了实用导论。CFMs是将机器学习中基础模型的范式（大规模预训练、无需微调的上下文学习）引入因果推断领域，通过在全新数据集上使用上下文学习来估计因果量（如平均处理效应），无需模型更新。文章总结了因果推断与机器学习所需背景知识，并附有示例代码和Jupyter notebooks。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果推断需要对每个新问题构建定制化的流水线（提出因果机制、选择合适估计器、训练模型），过程繁琐且缺乏可复用性。与此同时，机器学习已在多种模态中转向基础模型范式（大规模预训练、不微调即可应用），因此将这一范式引入因果推断以提升效率和通用性是自然的推进方向。

Method: 本文是一篇实用导论性论文，首先梳理因果推断和机器学习所需的背景知识，随后系统地介绍CFMs的核心概念——即经过预训练的神经网络，能够在新数据集上通过in-context learning（上下文学习）估计诸如平均处理效应等因果量，而无需进行模型更新。文中配有示例代码和Jupyter notebooks以辅助理解。

Result: 文章提供了对CFMs这一新兴领域的综合性实用介绍，涵盖其背景知识、核心机制（预训练+上下文学习+零微调），并通过示例代码和notebook为读者提供了实践入门的路径。

Conclusion: 因果基础模型将基础学习带动到因果推断中，有望取代传统问题定制化的因果推断流水线，成为一种通用、可扩展的因果估计范式。本文作为实用的入门导论，为研究者提供了理解与上手CFMs的基础资源和指引。

Abstract: Causal inference is the practice of estimating the effect of a treatment or intervention from data. It traditionally requires a bespoke pipeline for every new problem: first proposing a causal mechanism, selecting a compatible estimator, and finally training it. Meanwhile, across diverse settings and modalities, much of machine learning has shifted to the paradigm of foundation models: networks pretrained once at scale and applied to new tasks without fine-tuning. Causal foundation models (CFMs) bring this paradigm to causal inference. CFMs are pretrained neural networks that estimate causal quantities, such as the average treatment effect, on entirely new datasets using in-context learning without requiring model updates. This work provides a practical introduction to this emerging area. We summarize the necessary background in causal inference and machine learning before discussing CFMs. Throughout, we include example code and Jupyter notebooks.

</details>
